#### # Instructions  
####   
#### Given students' names along with the grade that they are in, create a roster for the school.  
####   
#### In the end, you should be able to:  
####   
#### - Add a student's name to the roster for a grade  
####   - "Add Jim to grade 2."  
####   - "OK."  
#### - Get a list of all students enrolled in a grade  
####   - "Which students are in grade 2?"  
####   - "We've only got Jim just now."  
#### - Get a sorted list of all students in all grades.  
####   Grades should sort as 1, 2, 3, etc., and students within a grade should be sorted alphabetically by name.  
####   - "Who all is enrolled in school right now?"  
####   - "Let me think.  
####     We have Anna, Barb, and Charlie in grade 1, Alex, Peter, and Zoe in grade 2 and Jim in grade 5.  
####     So the answer is: Anna, Barb, Charlie, Alex, Peter, Zoe and Jim"  
####   
#### Note that all our students only have one name (It's a small town, what do you want?) and each student cannot be added more than once to a grade or the roster.  
#### In fact, when a test attempts to add the same student more than once, your implementation should indicate that this is incorrect.  
####   
#### ####  
####   
#### Use the above instructions to modify the supplied files: grade_school.cpp grade_school.h  
#### Don't change the names of existing functions or classes, as they may be referenced from other code like unit tests, etc.  
#### Only use standard libraries, don't suggest installing any packages.  

grade_school.h
```
#if !defined(GRADE_SCHOOL_H)
#define GRADE_SCHOOL_H

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>

namespace grade_school {

class school {
private:
    std::map<int, std::vector<std::string>> _roster;

public:
    void add(std::string student, int grade);
    std::vector<std::string> roster();
    std::vector<std::string> studentsInGrade(int grade);
};

}  // namespace grade_school

#endif // GRADE_SCHOOL_H
```

grade_school.cpp
```
#include "grade_school.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace grade_school {

void school::add(std::string student, int grade) {
    // Check if the grade already exists in the map
    if (_roster.find(grade) == _roster.end()) {
        // If not, create it with the student in a new vector
        _roster[grade] = {student};
    } else {
        // If the grade exists, check if the student is already in that grade's list
        auto& students = _roster[grade];
        if (std::find(students.begin(), students.end(), student) == students.end()) {
            // If not, add the student
            students.push_back(student);
        }
        // If the student is already present, the request is incorrect (do nothing)
    }
}

std::vector<std::string> school::roster() {
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    // Iterate over the map. std::map automatically sorts keys.
    for (auto const& [grade, student_list] : _roster) {
        // Create a copy of the student list to sort it
        std::vector<std::string> sorted_students = student_list;
        std::sort(sorted_students.begin(), sorted_students.end());
        // Append the sorted students to the result list
        result.insert(result.end(), sorted_students.begin(), sorted_students.end());
    }
    return result;
}

std::vector<std::string> school::studentsInGrade(int grade) {
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    // Check if the grade exists
    if (_roster.find(grade) != _roster.end()) {
        result = _roster[grade];
        // Sort the students alphabetically for this grade
        std::sort(result.begin(), result.end());
    }
    // If the grade doesn't exist, return an empty vector
    return result;
}

}  // namespace grade_school
```

grade_school.h
```
#if !defined(GRADE_SCHOOL_H)
#define GRADE_SCHOOL_H

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>

namespace grade_school {

class school {
private:
    std::map<int, std::vector<std::string>> _roster;

public:
    void add(std::string student, int grade);
    std::map<int, std::vector<std::string>> roster() const;
    std::vector<std::string> studentsInGrade(int grade) const;
    std::vector<std::string> grade(int grade);
};

}  // namespace grade_school

#endif // GRADE_SCHOOL_H
```

grade_school.cpp
```
#include "grade_school.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace grade_school {

void school::add(std::string student, int grade) {
    if (_roster.find(grade) == _roster.end()) {
        _roster[grade] = {student};
    } else {
        auto& students = _roster[grade];
        if (std::find(students.begin(), students.end(), student) == students.end()) {
            students.push_back(student);
        }
    }
}

std::map<int, std::vector<std::string>> school::roster() const {
    return _roster;
}

std::vector<std::string> school::studentsInGrade(int grade) const {
    if (_roster.find(grade) != _roster.end()) {
        std::vector<std::string> result = _roster[grade];
        std::sort(result.begin(), result.end());
        return result;
    }
    return {};
}

std::vector<std::string> school::grade(int grade) const {
    return studentsInGrade(grade);
}

}  // namespace grade_school
```

The errors indicate that the `roster()` function is returning a `std::map` (which cannot be compared to a `std::vector`), and the `grade()` function is missing or not properly declared for use on const objects.

To fix these:
1.  Change the return type of `roster()` from `std::map` to `std::vector<std::string>` to return the flattened, sorted list of all students.
2.  Add `const` qualifiers to the `grade()` method in the header to match the expected usage on const objects and fix the "discards qualifiers" error.
3.  Implement `grade()` in the source file to simply return the result of `studentsInGrade()`.

#if !defined(GRADE_SCHOOL_H)
#define GRADE_SCHOOL_H

#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>

namespace grade_school {

class school {
private:
    std::map<int, std::vector<std::string>> _roster;

public:

    std::vector<std::string> roster() const;
    std::vector<std::string> studentsInGrade(int grade) const;

};

grade_school.cpp
```
#include "grade_school.h"
#include <algorithm>

namespace grade_school {

void school::add(std::string student, int grade) {
    if (_roster.find(grade) == _roster.end()) {
        _roster[grade] = {student};
    } else {
        auto& students = _roster[grade];
        if (std::find(students.begin(), students.end(), student) == students.end()) {
            students.push_back(student);
        }
    }
}

std::vector<std::string> school::roster() const {
    std::vector<std::string> result;
    for (const auto& pair : _roster) {
        std::vector<std::string> sorted_students = pair.second;
        std::sort(sorted_students.begin(), sorted_students.end());
        result.insert(result.end(), sorted_students.begin(), sorted_students.end());
    }
    return result;
}

std::vector<std::string> school::studentsInGrade(int grade) const {
    if (_roster.find(grade) != _roster.end()) {
        std::vector<std::string> result = _roster[grade];
        std::sort(result.begin(), result.end());
        return result;
    }
    return {};
}

std::vector<std::string> school::grade(int grade) const {
    return studentsInGrade(grade);
}

}  // namespace grade_school
```
